```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>达人账号分析系统 v3.0</title>
<style>
*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
body{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI","PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif;line-height:1.7;color:#333;background:#f0f2f5;padding:20px}
.container{max-width:1400px;margin:0 auto}
h1{color:#1a1a2e;font-size:24px;margin-bottom:4px}
.subtitle{color:#666;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:20px}

/* Input Panel */
.input-panel{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;margin-bottom:24px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}
.input-row{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.input-group{flex:1;min-width:200px}
.input-group label{display:block;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:4px}
.input-group input,.input-group select{width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-family:inherit}
.input-group input:focus,.input-group select:focus{outline:none;border-color:#4472C4;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(68,114,196,0.15)}
.btn{background:#4472C4;color:#fff;border:none;padding:10px 24px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap}
.btn:hover{background:#365899}
.btn:disabled{opacity:0.6;cursor:not-allowed}
.btn-secondary{background:#64748b}
.btn-secondary:hover{background:#475569}
.status{font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-top:8px;min-height:20px}
.status.error{color:#dc2626}
.status.ok{color:#059669}

/* Stats */
.stats{display:flex;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:24px}
.stat{background:#fff;border-radius:10px;padding:16px 24px;text-align:center;min-width:120px;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)}
.stat .num{font-size:28px;font-weight:700;color:#4472C4}
.stat .label{font-size:12px;color:#64748b;margin-top:2px}

/* Section */
.section{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;margin-bottom:24px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}
.section h2{font-size:18px;color:#1a1a2e;margin-bottom:16px;padding-bottom:8px;border-bottom:2px solid #e8ecf1}
.section h3{font-size:15px;color:#334155;margin:16px 0 10px}

/* Bar Chart */
.bar-chart{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:10px;height:200px;padding:20px 10px;background:#f8fafc;border-radius:8px;min-width:500px}
.bar{flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;min-width:70px}
.bar-fill{width:100%;border-radius:4px 4px 0 0;transition:all 0.3s;min-height:4px}
.bar:hover .bar-fill{filter:brightness(0.9)}
.bar-label{font-size:11px;color:#64748b;margin-top:6px;text-align:center;line-height:1.3;max-width:90px;word-break:break-all}
.bar-value{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#1a1a2e;margin-bottom:4px}

/* Table */
.video-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:12px 0;font-size:13px}
.video-table th{background:#1e293b;color:#fff;padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;position:sticky;top:0}
.video-table td{padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;vertical-align:top}
.video-table tr:nth-child(even){background:#f8fafc}
.video-table tr:hover{background:#e8f0fe}
.cat-badge{display:inline-block;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap}
.video-link{color:#4472C4;text-decoration:none;font-size:12px}
.video-link:hover{text-decoration:underline}

/* Comment Part Cards */
.comment-part{margin-bottom:24px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden}
.comment-part-header{background:#f8fafc;padding:16px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}
.comment-part-header h3{margin:0;font-size:16px}
.comment-part-body{padding:20px}

/* Video cards in comment part */
.video-cards{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:16px}
.video-card{flex:1;min-width:220px;background:#f8fafc;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;padding:14px;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.2s}
.video-card:hover{border-color:#4472C4;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(68,114,196,0.12)}
.video-card.active{border-color:#4472C4;background:#eef2ff}
.video-card .vc-title{font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a2e;margin-bottom:6px;line-height:1.4;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:2;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden}
.video-card .vc-meta{font-size:12px;color:#64748b}
.video-card .vc-count{font-size:20px;font-weight:700;color:#4472C4}
.video-card .vc-toggle{font-size:11px;color:#4472C4;margin-top:6px;cursor:pointer}

/* Comment detail list */
.comment-detail{margin-top:12px;padding:12px;background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;display:none}
.comment-detail.show{display:block}
.comment-item{padding:6px 0;font-size:13px;border-bottom:1px dashed #e2e8f0}
.comment-item:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.comment-cat{display:inline-block;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;margin-right:6px;font-weight:600}
.c-inquiry{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
.c-intent{background:#ffedd5;color:#c2410c}
.c-discuss{background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e}
.c-positive{background:#d1fae5;color:#065f46}
.c-negative{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
.c-social{background:#dbeafe;color:#1e40af}

/* Purchase intent badge */
.intent-badge{display:inline-block;padding:4px 14px;border-radius:20px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;margin:8px 0}
.intent-high{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
.intent-medium{background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e}
.intent-low{background:#d1fae5;color:#065f46}

/* Loading */
.loading{text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#64748b}
.spinner{display:inline-block;width:36px;height:36px;border:3px solid #e2e8f0;border-top-color:#4472C4;border-radius:50%;animation:spin 0.8s linear infinite;margin-bottom:8px}
@keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
.progress-bar{width:100%;height:6px;background:#e2e8f0;border-radius:3px;margin:12px 0;overflow:hidden}
.progress-fill{height:100%;background:#4472C4;border-radius:3px;transition:width 0.3s}

/* Filter bar */
.filter-bar{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}
.filter-bar select,.filter-bar input{padding:8px 12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;font-family:inherit}
.filter-bar input{min-width:200px}
.filter-bar select:focus,.filter-bar input:focus{outline:none;border-color:#4472C4}

/* Download */
.download-btn{display:inline-block;background:#4472C4;color:#fff;padding:10px 20px;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:none;font-size:14px;margin-right:8px}
.download-btn:hover{background:#365899}

/* Error/Empty */
.empty-state{text-align:center;padding:60px 20px;color:#94a3b8}
.empty-state .icon{font-size:48px;margin-bottom:12px}
.error-box{background:#fef2f2;border:1px solid #fecaca;color:#991b1b;padding:12px 16px;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;margin:12px 0}

.hidden{display:none}
.flex{display:flex}
.gap-8{gap:8px}
.mt-8{margin-top:8px}
.mb-12{margin-bottom:12px}
.text-sm{font-size:12px;color:#64748b}

/* Sidebar + Panel Layout */
.layout{display:flex;gap:20px;align-items:flex-start}
.sidebar{width:220px;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:16px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08);position:sticky;top:20px;flex-shrink:0}
.sidebar h3{font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px;font-weight:700}
.nav-btn{width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:none;padding:12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;color:#334155;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;transition:all 0.2s}
.nav-btn:hover{background:#f1f5f9}
.nav-btn.active{background:#eef2ff;color:#4472C4;font-weight:600}
.main-content{flex:1;min-width:0}
.panel-header{margin-bottom:20px}
.panel-header h1{margin-bottom:4px}

/* Single video results */
.transcript-box{background:#f8fafc;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;padding:16px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;max-height:300px;overflow-y:auto;white-space:pre-wrap}
.analysis-card{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px}
.analysis-card h4{font-size:14px;color:#1a1a2e;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px}
.analysis-card p{font-size:13px;color:#475569;line-height:1.7}
.meta-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(140px,1fr));gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px}
.meta-item{background:#f8fafc;border-radius:8px;padding:12px;text-align:center}
.meta-item .num{font-size:20px;font-weight:700;color:#4472C4}
.meta-item .label{font-size:12px;color:#64748b}

/* Modal */
.modal-overlay{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:1000}
.modal-overlay.hidden{display:none}
.modal{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;width:90%;max-width:500px;max-height:90vh;overflow-y:auto;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)}
.modal h2{margin-bottom:16px;font-size:18px}
.modal .input-group{margin-bottom:16px}
.modal .input-group label{font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b;display:block;margin-bottom:4px}
.modal .input-group input{width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px}
.modal .input-group .hint{font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:4px}
.modal-actions{display:flex;gap:12px;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:20px}
.key-status{font-size:12px;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:4px;display:inline-block;margin-left:8px}
.key-status.ok{background:#d1fae5;color:#065f46}
.key-status.missing{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="container layout">

<!-- Sidebar -->
<aside class="sidebar">
    <h3>📊 分析看板</h3>
    <button class="nav-btn active" id="nav-1" onclick="switchPanel(1)">
        <span>1</span> 选题 &amp; 评论总盘
    </button>
    <button class="nav-btn" id="nav-2" onclick="switchPanel(2)">
        <span>2</span> 单条视频深入分析
    </button>
    <button class="nav-btn" id="nav-3" onclick="switchPanel(3)">
        <span>3</span> 数据更新入口
    </button>
    <div style="margin-top:24px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0">
        <button class="nav-btn" onclick="openSettingsModal()" style="color:#64748b">
            <span>⚙️</span> API 设置
        </button>
    </div>
</aside>

<!-- Main Content -->
<main class="main-content">

<div class="panel-header">
    <h1>📊 达人账号分析系统 v3.0</h1>
    <p class="subtitle" id="page-subtitle">输入任意达人ID → 选题分布 + 双维度评论分析 → 定位细分赛道 + 评估购买意向</p>
</div>

<!-- Panel 1: Existing Influencer Analysis -->
<div id="panel-1" class="panel">

<!-- Input Panel -->
<div class="input-panel">
<div class="input-row">
<div class="input-group">
<label>📱 平台</label>
<select id="platform"><option value="Instagram">Instagram</option><option value="TikTok">TikTok</option></select>
</div>
<div class="input-group">
<label>🌐 语言</label>
<select id="language"><option value="en" selected>英语</option><option value="es">西语</option></select>
</div>
<div class="input-group" style="flex:3">
<label>📥 达人链接或ID <span style="font-weight:400;color:#64748b;font-size:11px">（多个用逗号/换行分隔）</span></label>
<textarea id="input-url" placeholder="例如: @letsdodizz, @imparkerburton&#10;或一行一个达人ID" rows="2" style="width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-family:inherit;resize:vertical"></textarea>
</div>
<div class="input-group">
<label>📅 采集天数</label>
<select id="fetch-days">
<option value="7">7天</option><option value="14">14天</option><option value="30" selected>30天</option><option value="60">60天</option><option value="90">90天</option>
</select>
</div>
<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
<button class="btn" id="analyze-btn" onclick="analyzeInfluencer()">🔍 单个分析</button>
<button class="btn btn-secondary" id="batch-btn" onclick="batchAnalyze()">🚀 批量分析</button>
</div>
</div>
<div class="status" id="status"></div>
<div id="batch-progress" class="hidden" style="margin-top:12px">
<div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" id="batch-progress-fill" style="width:0%"></div></div>
<p class="text-sm" id="batch-progress-text"></p>
<button class="btn btn-secondary" id="batch-stop-btn" onclick="stopBatchAnalyze()" style="margin-top:8px">⏹ 停止分析</button>
</div>
</div>

<!-- Results Area -->
<div id="results-area" class="hidden">

<!-- Stats -->
<div id="stats-row" class="stats"></div>

<!-- Topic Distribution -->
<div id="topic-section" class="section">
<h2>📊 选题分布</h2>
<div id="topic-chart" class="bar-chart"></div>
</div>

<!-- Video Detail -->
<div id="video-section" class="section">
<h2>📋 视频明细</h2>
<div class="filter-bar">
<select id="filter-cat"><option value="">全部分类</option></select>
<input type="text" id="filter-kw" placeholder="🔎 搜索标题关键词..." oninput="applyFilters()">
<select id="filter-sort" onchange="applyFilters()">
<option value="date-desc">发布日期(新→旧)</option>
<option value="comments-desc">评论数(高→低)</option>
<option value="category">分类</option>
</select>
</div>
<div id="video-list"></div>
</div>

<!-- Comment Analysis — Two Parts -->
<div id="comment-analysis-section" class="section">
<h2>💬 评论分析（ScrapeCreators 实时抓取）</h2>
<div id="comment-analysis-container">
<div class="loading" id="comment-loading">
<div class="spinner"></div>
<p>正在从 ScrapeCreators 抓取评论...</p>
<div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" id="comment-progress" style="width:0%"></div></div>
<p class="text-sm" id="comment-progress-text">等待开始...</p>
<button class="btn btn-secondary" id="comment-stop-btn" onclick="stopCommentFetch()" style="margin-top:12px">⏹ 停止抓取</button>
</div>
<div id="comment-parts" class="hidden"></div>
</div>
</div>

<!-- Download -->
<div id="download-section" class="section">
<h2>📥 下载报告</h2>
<button class="download-btn" onclick="downloadExcel()">📥 下载完整分析数据 (Excel)</button>
</div>

</div>
</div>

<!-- Panel 2: Single Video Deep Analysis -->
<div id="panel-2" class="panel hidden">
    <div class="input-panel">
        <h2 style="margin-bottom:16px">🎬 单条视频深入分析</h2>
        <div class="input-row">
            <div class="input-group" style="flex:3">
                <label>🎥 视频链接 <span style="font-weight:400;color:#64748b;font-size:11px">（TikTok / Instagram 任意视频）</span></label>
                <input type="text" id="video-url-input" placeholder="例如: https://www.tiktok.com/@username/video/1234567890" style="width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px">
            </div>
            <div class="input-group">
                <label>📱 平台</label>
                <select id="video-platform-input"><option value="">自动识别</option><option value="TikTok">TikTok</option><option value="Instagram">Instagram</option></select>
            </div>
            <div class="input-group">
                <label>🌐 语言</label>
                <select id="video-language-input"><option value="en" selected>英语</option><option value="es">西语</option></select>
            </div>
            <button class="btn" id="video-analyze-btn" onclick="analyzeSingleVideo()" style="margin-bottom:0">开始分析</button>
        </div>
        <div class="status" id="video-status"></div>
    </div>
    <div id="video-results-area" class="hidden"></div>
</div>

<!-- Panel 3: Bulk Video View Update -->
<div id="panel-3" class="panel hidden">
    <div class="input-panel">
        <h2 style="margin-bottom:16px">📤 批量视频 View 更新</h2>
        <p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:12px">从 Excel 整列复制粘贴（含空单元格）。TK 列决定总行数，IG 列不足自动在前面补空行。抓取完成后一键复制 View 列粘贴回 Excel。</p>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:16px;margin-bottom:12px">
            <div style="flex:1">
                <label style="display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;color:#475569">🎵 TikTok 链接（从 A 列复制）</label>
                <textarea id="paste-tk-urls" placeholder="https://www.tiktok.com/@user/video/123&#10;https://www.tiktok.com/@user/video/456" rows="8" style="width:100%;padding:8px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-family:monospace;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box"></textarea>
            </div>
            <div style="flex:1">
                <label style="display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;color:#475569">📸 Instagram 链接（从 B 列复制）</label>
                <textarea id="paste-ig-urls" placeholder="https://www.instagram.com/reel/ABC/&#10;https://www.instagram.com/reel/DEF/" rows="8" style="width:100%;padding:8px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-family:monospace;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box"></textarea>
            </div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn" id="views-paste-btn" onclick="fetchPasteViews()" style="margin-top:8px">🔍 开始抓取 View</button>
        <div class="status" id="views-status"></div>
    </div>
    <div id="views-results-area" class="hidden"></div>
</div>

</main>
</div>

<!-- API Settings Modal -->
<div id="settings-modal" class="modal-overlay hidden">
    <div class="modal" style="max-width:520px">
        <h2>⚙️ API Key 设置</h2>
        <p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:16px">支持多个 Key（英文逗号分隔），第一个用完自动切换下一个。</p>

        <div class="input-group">
            <label>ScrapeCreators API Key <span id="sc-status" class="key-status missing">未设置</span></label>
            <input type="text" id="sc-key-input" placeholder="key1,key2,key3（多个用逗号分隔）">
            <div class="hint">用于视频数据、View 抓取、口播转录 | <span id="sc-credits" style="color:#4472C4"></span></div>
        </div>

        <div class="input-group">
            <label>Apify API Key <span id="apify-status" class="key-status missing">未设置</span></label>
            <input type="text" id="apify-key-input" placeholder="粘贴 Apify API Key">
            <div class="hint">用于抓取达人视频列表 | <span id="apify-credits" style="color:#4472C4"></span></div>
        </div>

        <div class="input-group">
            <label>DeepSeek API Key <span id="deepseek-status" class="key-status missing">未设置</span></label>
            <input type="text" id="deepseek-key-input" placeholder="粘贴 DeepSeek API Key">
            <div class="hint">用于 AI 内容拆解</div>
        </div>

        <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeSettingsModal()">取消</button>
            <button class="btn" onclick="refreshCredits()" style="background:#059669">🔄 刷新额度</button>
            <button class="btn" onclick="saveApiKeys()">保存并更新</button>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
// ============ State ============
let allVideos = [];
let currentUsername = '';
let currentPlatform = '';
let commentAnalysisData = null;
let commentAbortController = null;  // For stopping comment fetch
let batchAbortController = null;    // For stopping batch analyze

const CAT_COLORS = {
    "3C配件品牌赞助/种草":"#F8CBAD","Apple/iOS生态":"#DDEBF7","其他品牌产品种草":"#FCE4D6",
    "科技资讯/教程技巧":"#D9E1F2","AI工具/生活观点":"#E2EFDA","赞助广告内容":"#F4CCCC","其他":"#F2F2F2"
};

const COMMENT_CAT_INFO = {
    "purchase_inquiry": ["产品咨询","c-inquiry","#FF6B6B"],
    "purchase_intent": ["购买意向","c-intent","#FFD93D"],
    "product_discussion": ["产品讨论","c-discuss","#6BCB77"],
    "positive_feedback": ["正向反馈","c-positive","#4D96FF"],
    "negative_feedback": ["负向反馈","c-negative","#E74C3C"],
    "social_engagement": ["社交互动","c-social","#9B59B6"],
    "other": ["其他","","#95A5A6"]
};

const SPONSORED_CATS = ["3C配件品牌赞助/种草","其他品牌产品种草","赞助广告内容"];

// ============ Main Flow ============
function parseInputs(text) {
    // 支持逗号、换行、空格分隔
    return text.split(/[,，\n]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s.length > 0);
}

async function analyzeInfluencer() {
    const rawInput = document.getElementById('input-url').value.trim();
    const inputs = parseInputs(rawInput);
    const input = inputs[0] || '';
    const platform = document.getElementById('platform').value;
    const days = document.getElementById('fetch-days').value;
    const language = document.getElementById('language').value;
    const status = document.getElementById('status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('analyze-btn');

    if (!input) { status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">请输入达人账号</span>'; return; }

    btn.disabled = true;
    status.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在读取达人资源库...';

    try {
        // Step 1: Get video list + classification
        const resp = await fetch('/api/analyze', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({input, platform, days, language})
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (data.error) { status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">${data.error}</span>`; btn.disabled = false; return; }

        allVideos = data.videos || [];
        currentUsername = data.username;
        currentPlatform = data.platform;

        // 缓存最新 → 直接显示
        if (data.source === 'cache' && allVideos.length) {
            renderTopicAndVideos();
            document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">${data.status || '✅ 缓存最新，共 ' + allVideos.length + ' 条视频'}</span>`;
            btn.disabled = false;
            await fetchCommentAnalysis();
            return;
        }

        if (!allVideos.length) {
            if (data.need_apify) {
                status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">⚠️ ${data.message}</span>`;
                document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
                const cachedInfo = data.cached_count ? `<p style="color:#059669">📦 已有 ${data.cached_count} 条历史缓存，抓取后将自动去重合并</p>` : '';
                document.getElementById('stats-row').innerHTML = `
                <div class="empty-state" style="width:100%">
                    <div class="icon">🔌</div>
                    <h3>需要 Apify 数据抓取</h3>
                    <p style="margin:12px 0">${data.message}</p>
                    ${cachedInfo}
                    <div class="mt-8 flex gap-8" style="justify-content:center">
                        <button class="btn" onclick="triggerApifyAuto()">🔑 自动抓取（Apify Key 已配置）</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showUploadInput()">📤 上传视频数据</button>
                    </div>
                    <div id="upload-input-area" class="hidden mt-8" style="max-width:500px;margin:12px auto">
                        <textarea id="upload-json" placeholder='粘贴 JSON 视频列表 [{title,date,url,comments_count},...]' style="width:100%;height:100px;padding:8px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:8px"></textarea>
                        <button class="btn" onclick="uploadVideos()">上传数据</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
                document.getElementById('topic-section').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('video-section').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('comment-analysis-section').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('download-section').classList.add('hidden');
            }
            btn.disabled = false;
            return;
        }

        // Step 2: Render topic + video table
        renderTopicAndVideos();
        document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
        status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ 分析完成: ${allVideos.length} 条视频 | @${currentUsername} (${currentPlatform}) [${data.source}]</span>`;

        // Step 3: Auto-fetch comment analysis
        await fetchCommentAnalysis();

    } catch(e) {
        status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">请求失败: ${e.message}</span>`;
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// ============ Topic + Video Rendering ============
function renderTopicAndVideos() {
    if (!allVideos.length) return;

    // Stats
    const cats = {};
    let totalComments = 0;
    allVideos.forEach(v => {
        cats[v['分类']] = (cats[v['分类']] || 0) + 1;
        totalComments += parseInt(v['评论数']) || 0;
    });
    const total = allVideos.length;
    const hitRate = 100 - ((cats['其他'] || 0) / total * 100);
    const avgComments = Math.round(totalComments / total);
    const sponsoredCount = allVideos.filter(v => SPONSORED_CATS.includes(v['分类'])).length;

    document.getElementById('stats-row').innerHTML = `
        <div class="stat"><div class="num">${total}</div><div class="label">视频总数</div></div>
        <div class="stat"><div class="num">@${currentUsername}</div><div class="label">达人</div></div>
        <div class="stat"><div class="num">${hitRate.toFixed(0)}%</div><div class="label">分类命中率</div></div>
        <div class="stat"><div class="num">${avgComments}</div><div class="label">平均评论</div></div>
        <div class="stat"><div class="num">${sponsoredCount}</div><div class="label">赞助/种草</div></div>
    `;

    // Topic bar chart
    const sortedCats = Object.entries(cats).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    const maxCount = Math.max(...sortedCats.map(c => c[1]), 1);
    let barHTML = '';
    sortedCats.forEach(([cat, count]) => {
        const pct = (count / total * 100).toFixed(1);
        const color = CAT_COLORS[cat] || '#F2F2F2';
        const height = Math.max((count / maxCount) * 160, 8);
        barHTML += `<div class="bar"><div class="bar-value">${count} (${pct}%)</div><div class="bar-fill" style="height:${height}px;background:${color}"></div><div class="bar-label">${cat}</div></div>`;
    });
    document.getElementById('topic-chart').innerHTML = barHTML;
    document.getElementById('topic-section').classList.remove('hidden');

    // Category filter
    const filterCat = document.getElementById('filter-cat');
    filterCat.innerHTML = '<option value="">全部分类</option>' + sortedCats.map(([cat]) => `<option value="${cat}">${cat}</option>`).join('');

    // Video table
    renderVideoList();
    document.getElementById('video-section').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('download-section').classList.remove('hidden');
}

function renderVideoList() {
    const catFilter = document.getElementById('filter-cat').value;
    const kwFilter = document.getElementById('filter-kw').value.toLowerCase();
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('filter-sort').value;

    let videos = [...allVideos];
    if (catFilter) videos = videos.filter(v => v['分类'] === catFilter);
    if (kwFilter) videos = videos.filter(v => (v['标题']||'').toLowerCase().includes(kwFilter));
    if (sortBy === 'comments-desc') videos.sort((a,b) => (b['评论数']||0) - (a['评论数']||0));
    else if (sortBy === 'category') videos.sort((a,b) => (a['分类']||'').localeCompare(b['分类']||''));
    else videos.sort((a,b) => (b['发布日期']||'').localeCompare(a['发布日期']||''));

    let html = `<p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:8px">显示 ${videos.length} / ${allVideos.length} 条</p>`;
    html += `<table class="video-table"><thead><tr><th>发布日期</th><th>分类</th><th>标题</th><th>评论</th><th>链接</th><th>命中品牌</th></tr></thead><tbody>`;

    videos.forEach(v => {
        const cat = v['分类'] || '其他';
        const color = CAT_COLORS[cat] || '#F2F2F2';
        const title = (v['标题']||'').substring(0, 120) + ((v['标题']||'').length > 120 ? '...' : '');
        const url = v['视频链接'] || '';
        html += `<tr>
<td>${v['发布日期']||''}</td>
<td><span class="cat-badge" style="background:${color}">${cat.substring(0,14)}</span></td>
<td>${escHtml(title)}</td>
<td>${v['评论数']||0}</td>
<td>${url ? `<a href="${escHtml(url)}" class="video-link" target="_blank" rel="noopener">🔗 打开</a>` : '-'}</td>
<td>${v['命中品牌']||'-'}</td>
</tr>`;
    });
    html += '</tbody></table>';
    document.getElementById('video-list').innerHTML = html;
}

function applyFilters() { renderVideoList(); }
document.getElementById('filter-cat').addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
document.getElementById('filter-sort').addEventListener('change', applyFilters);

// ============ Comment Analysis ============
async function fetchCommentAnalysis() {
    const container = document.getElementById('comment-analysis-section');
    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('comment-loading');
    const partsEl = document.getElementById('comment-parts');
    const progressFill = document.getElementById('comment-progress');
    const progressText = document.getElementById('comment-progress-text');
    const stopBtn = document.getElementById('comment-stop-btn');

    // Guard: 没有视频数据则跳过
    if (!allVideos || !allVideos.length) {
        console.warn('[comments] allVideos is empty, skipping comment analysis');
        container.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }

    // Abort any existing fetch
    if (commentAbortController) {
        commentAbortController.abort();
    }
    commentAbortController = new AbortController();

    container.classList.remove('hidden');
    loadingEl.classList.remove('hidden');
    partsEl.classList.add('hidden');
    progressFill.style.width = '0%';
    progressText.textContent = '正在连接 ScrapeCreators...';
    stopBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    stopBtn.textContent = '⏹ 停止抓取';
    stopBtn.disabled = false;

    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/comments/analysis', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({videos: allVideos, platform: currentPlatform}),
            signal: commentAbortController.signal
        });

        // Simulate progress while waiting
        let progress = 0;
        const progressInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (commentAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
            progress = Math.min(progress + Math.random() * 8, 90);
            progressFill.style.width = progress + '%';
            progressText.textContent = `正在抓取评论... (${Math.round(progress)}%)`;
        }, 400);

        const data = await resp.json();
        clearInterval(progressInterval);

        if (commentAbortController.signal.aborted) return;

        progressFill.style.width = '100%';
        progressText.textContent = '分类完成 ✓';
        stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');

        if (data.error) {
            loadingEl.innerHTML = `<div class="error-box">评论抓取失败: ${data.error}</div>`;
            return;
        }

        commentAnalysisData = data;
        setTimeout(() => {
            loadingEl.classList.add('hidden');
            partsEl.classList.remove('hidden');
            renderCommentParts(data);
        }, 300);

    } catch(e) {
        if (e.name === 'AbortError') {
            loadingEl.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state" style="padding:20px"><p>⏹ 已停止评论抓取</p><p class="text-sm">评论分析已中止</p></div>`;
            stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');
            return;
        }
        loadingEl.innerHTML = `<div class="error-box">请求失败: ${e.message}</div>`;
    }
}

function stopCommentFetch() {
    if (commentAbortController) {
        commentAbortController.abort();
        commentAbortController = null;
        document.getElementById('comment-stop-btn').textContent = '已停止';
        document.getElementById('comment-stop-btn').disabled = true;
    }
}

function renderCommentParts(data) {
    const partsEl = document.getElementById('comment-parts');
    let html = '';

    // Part 1: TOP-3 Hot Videos
    html += renderCommentPart(data.part1_top3_hot, 1, '🔥 TOP-3 热门视频评论分布', '按评论数降序选取');

    // Part 2: TOP-3 Sponsored Videos
    html += renderCommentPart(data.part2_top3_sponsored, 2, '💼 TOP-3 赞助/种草视频评论分布', '按赞助/种草分类中评论数降序选取');

    partsEl.innerHTML = html;

    // Auto-expand first video in each part
    setTimeout(() => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.video-card').forEach((card, i) => {
            if (i % 3 === 0) card.click();
        });
    }, 100);
}

function renderCommentPart(partData, partNum, title, subtitle) {
    if (!partData || !partData.videos || !partData.videos.length) {
        return `<div class="comment-part"><div class="comment-part-header"><h3>${title}</h3></div><div class="comment-part-body"><p class="text-sm">暂无数据</p></div></div>`;
    }

    let html = `<div class="comment-part">
    <div class="comment-part-header">
        <div><h3>${title}</h3><span class="text-sm">${subtitle}</span></div>
        <span class="intent-badge intent-${partData.purchase_intent_label.includes('高')?'high':partData.purchase_intent_label.includes('中')?'medium':'low'}">
            🛒 ${partData.purchase_intent_label} (${partData.purchase_intent_score})
        </span>
    </div>
    <div class="comment-part-body">`;

    // Video cards
    html += '<div class="video-cards">';
    partData.videos.forEach((v, i) => {
        const vid = `p${partNum}-v${i}`;
        const hasComments = v.classified_comments && v.classified_comments.length > 0;
        html += `<div class="video-card" id="card-${vid}" onclick="toggleCommentDetail('${vid}')">
            <div class="vc-count">${v.native_comment_count}</div>
            <div class="vc-meta">条评论</div>
            <div class="vc-title">${escHtml(v.title.substring(0,80))}</div>
            <div class="vc-meta">抓取: ${v.fetched_comment_count} 条有效评论</div>
            ${v.fetch_error ? `<div class="vc-meta" style="color:#dc2626">⚠️ ${v.fetch_error}</div>` : ''}
            <div class="vc-toggle">${hasComments ? '▼ 展开评论' : '无评论数据'}</div>
        </div>`;
    });
    html += '</div>';

    // Comment detail lists (hidden by default)
    partData.videos.forEach((v, i) => {
        const vid = `p${partNum}-v${i}`;
        html += `<div class="comment-detail" id="detail-${vid}">`;
        if (v.classified_comments && v.classified_comments.length > 0) {
            html += `<p class="text-sm mb-12">📝 ${v.classified_comments.length} 条分类评论 (共抓取 ${v.total_raw_comments} 条)</p>`;
            v.classified_comments.forEach(c => {
                const [catName, catCls] = COMMENT_CAT_INFO[c.category_key] || [c.category,''];
                html += `<div class="comment-item">
                    <span class="comment-cat ${catCls}">${catName}</span>
                    👍 ${c.likes} @${escHtml(c.username||'匿名')} — ${escHtml((c.text||'').substring(0,120))}
                </div>`;
            });
        } else if (v.fetch_error) {
            html += `<p class="error-box">抓取失败: ${v.fetch_error}</p>`;
        } else {
            html += '<p class="text-sm">无有效评论</p>';
        }
        html += '</div>';
    });

    // Aggregate distribution bar chart
    html += '<h3 style="margin-top:20px">📊 汇总评论分类分布</h3>';
    html += renderDistBarChart(partData.aggregate_distribution, partData.total_classified_comments);

    html += '</div></div>';
    return html;
}

function renderDistBarChart(distribution, total) {
    if (!distribution || !total) return '<p class="text-sm">暂无评论数据</p>';

    const entries = Object.entries(distribution).sort((a,b) => b[1].count - a[1].count);
    const maxCount = Math.max(...entries.map(e => e[1].count), 1);

    let html = '<div class="bar-chart">';
    entries.forEach(([key, data]) => {
        const [catName, catCls, catColor] = COMMENT_CAT_INFO[key] || [key, '', '#95A5A6'];
        const height = Math.max((data.count / maxCount) * 160, 8);
        html += `<div class="bar">
            <div class="bar-value">${data.count} (${data.pct}%)</div>
            <div class="bar-fill" style="height:${height}px;background:${catColor}"></div>
            <div class="bar-label">${catName}</div>
        </div>`;
    });
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
}

function toggleCommentDetail(vid) {
    const detail = document.getElementById('detail-' + vid);
    const card = document.getElementById('card-' + vid);
    const allDetails = document.querySelectorAll('.comment-detail');
    const allCards = document.querySelectorAll('.video-card');

    // Close all other details
    allDetails.forEach(d => { if (d.id !== 'detail-'+vid) d.classList.remove('show'); });
    allCards.forEach(c => { if (c.id !== 'card-'+vid) c.classList.remove('active'); });

    // Toggle current
    const isOpen = detail.classList.contains('show');
    if (isOpen) {
        detail.classList.remove('show');
        card.classList.remove('active');
        card.querySelector('.vc-toggle').textContent = '▼ 展开评论';
    } else {
        detail.classList.add('show');
        card.classList.add('active');
        card.querySelector('.vc-toggle').textContent = '▲ 收起评论';
    }
}

// ============ Apify / Upload helpers ============
function showUploadInput() {
    document.getElementById('upload-input-area').classList.toggle('hidden');
}

let apifyAbortController = null;  // For stopping Apify fetch

async function triggerApifyAuto() {
    const status = document.getElementById('status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('analyze-btn');
    btn.disabled = true;
    status.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在通过 Apify 抓取最新数据（自动去重合并）...';

    // 显示停止按钮
    const stopBtn = document.getElementById('apify-stop-btn');
    if (stopBtn) stopBtn.classList.remove('hidden');

    apifyAbortController = new AbortController();
    const startTime = Date.now();

    try {
        // Step 1: 启动异步任务（立即返回 task_id）
        const resp = await fetch('/api/apify/fetch', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            signal: apifyAbortController.signal,
            body: JSON.stringify({
                username: currentUsername,
                platform: currentPlatform,
                days: parseInt(document.getElementById('fetch-days').value),
                language: document.getElementById('language').value
            })
        });
        if (apifyAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
        const initData = await resp.json();
        if (initData.error) { renderError('status', initData.error, 'Apify'); btn.disabled = false; return; }

        const taskId = initData.task_id;
        // Step 2: 每 3 秒轮询状态（每次请求 <2s，永不超时）
        let elapsed = 0;
        while (true) {
            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
            elapsed += 3;
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> Apify 抓取中... (已等待 ${elapsed}s) <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="margin-left:8px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px" onclick="stopApifyFetch()">停止</button>`;
            const pollResp = await fetch('/api/apify/status/' + taskId, {signal: apifyAbortController.signal});
            if (apifyAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
            const pollData = await pollResp.json();
            if (pollData.status === 'done') {
                const costSec = Math.round((Date.now() - startTime) / 1000);
                allVideos = pollData.result.videos || [];
                renderTopicAndVideos();
                document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
                status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ ${pollData.result.message}（耗时 ${costSec}s）</span>`;
                await fetchCommentAnalysis();
                break;
            } else if (pollData.status === 'error') {
                renderError('status', pollData.error, 'Apify');
                break;
            }
            // status === 'started' or 'running' → continue polling
            if (elapsed > 120) {
                status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">抓取超时（>120s），请重试</span>';
                break;
            }
        }
    } catch(e) {
        if (apifyAbortController.signal.aborted) {
            status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">Apify 抓取已停止</span>';
        } else {
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">Apify 请求失败: ${e.message}</span>`;
        }
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        if (stopBtn) stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        apifyAbortController = null;
    }
}

function stopApifyFetch() {
    if (apifyAbortController) {
        apifyAbortController.abort();
        apifyAbortController = null;
    }
}

// ============ Batch Analysis ============
async function batchAnalyze() {
    const rawInput = document.getElementById('input-url').value.trim();
    const inputs = parseInputs(rawInput);
    const platform = document.getElementById('platform').value;
    const days = document.getElementById('fetch-days').value;
    const language = document.getElementById('language').value;
    const status = document.getElementById('status');
    const batchBtn = document.getElementById('batch-btn');
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('batch-progress');
    const progressFill = document.getElementById('batch-progress-fill');
    const progressText = document.getElementById('batch-progress-text');
    const stopBtn = document.getElementById('batch-stop-btn');

    if (!inputs.length) { status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">请输入达人账号</span>'; return; }

    // Abort any existing batch
    if (batchAbortController) {
        batchAbortController.abort();
    }
    batchAbortController = new AbortController();

    batchBtn.disabled = true;
    status.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在检查 ${inputs.length} 个达人的缓存状态...`;
    progressBar.classList.remove('hidden');
    stopBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    stopBtn.textContent = '⏹ 停止分析';
    stopBtn.disabled = false;

    try {
        // Step 1: Check cache for all
        const checkResp = await fetch('/api/batch/analyze', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({inputs, platform, days, language}),
            signal: batchAbortController.signal
        });

        if (batchAbortController.signal.aborted) return;

        const checkData = await checkResp.json();

        const cachedUsers = checkData.cached || [];
        const needApifyUsers = checkData.need_apify || [];
        const total = checkData.total || inputs.length;

        progressText.textContent = `${cachedUsers.length} 个有缓存，${needApifyUsers.length} 个需抓取`;
        progressFill.style.width = '30%';

        // Step 2: Fetch missing ones via Apify
        if (needApifyUsers.length > 0) {
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在抓取 ${needApifyUsers.length} 个达人...`;
            progressText.textContent = `正在 Apify 抓取中... (0/${needApifyUsers.length})`;

            const fetchResp = await fetch('/api/batch/fetch', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    usernames: needApifyUsers.map(u => u.username),
                    platform, days, language
                }),
                signal: batchAbortController.signal
            });

            if (batchAbortController.signal.aborted) return;

            const fetchData = await fetchResp.json();
            progressFill.style.width = '80%';
            progressText.textContent = `抓取完成: ${fetchData.total_done} 成功, ${fetchData.total_error} 失败`;

            // Merge cached results
            allVideos = [];
            for (const r of fetchData.results) {
                if (r.videos && r.videos.length) {
                    allVideos.push(...r.videos);
                }
            }
        }

        // Step 3: Load cached videos for already-cached users
        if (cachedUsers.length > 0 && needApifyUsers.length === 0) {
            // All cached — load from cache
            allVideos = [];
            for (const cu of cachedUsers) {
                if (batchAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
                const resp = await fetch('/api/analyze', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                    body: JSON.stringify({input: cu.username, platform, days, language})
                });
                const data = await resp.json();
                if (data.videos) allVideos.push(...data.videos);
            }
        }

        progressFill.style.width = '100%';
        progressText.textContent = `✅ 全部完成: ${allVideos.length} 条视频`;
        stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');

        if (allVideos.length > 0) {
            renderTopicAndVideos();
            document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ 批量分析完成: ${inputs.length} 个达人, ${allVideos.length} 条视频</span>`;
        } else {
            status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">所有达人均无缓存数据，请先单个抓取</span>';
        }
    } catch(e) {
        if (e.name === 'AbortError') {
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">⏹ 已停止批量分析</span>`;
            progressText.textContent = '已停止';
            stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');
            return;
        }
        status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">批量分析失败: ${e.message}</span>`;
    } finally {
        batchBtn.disabled = false;
        progressBar.classList.add('hidden');
        stopBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    }
}

function stopBatchAnalyze() {
    if (batchAbortController) {
        batchAbortController.abort();
        batchAbortController = null;
        document.getElementById('batch-stop-btn').textContent = '已停止';
        document.getElementById('batch-stop-btn').disabled = true;
    }
}

async function uploadVideos() {
    const jsonStr = document.getElementById('upload-json').value.trim();
    if (!jsonStr) { alert('请粘贴 JSON 数据'); return; }

    let videos;
    try { videos = JSON.parse(jsonStr); } catch(e) { alert('JSON 格式错误'); return; }
    if (!Array.isArray(videos)) { alert('JSON 应为数组'); return; }

    const status = document.getElementById('status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('analyze-btn');
    btn.disabled = true;
    status.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在上传数据...';

    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/upload/videos', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({username: currentUsername, platform: currentPlatform, videos})
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (data.error) { status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">${data.error}</span>`; btn.disabled = false; return; }

        allVideos = data.videos || [];
        renderTopicAndVideos();
        status.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ ${data.message}</span>`;
        await fetchCommentAnalysis();
    } catch(e) {
        status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">上传失败: ${e.message}</span>`;
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// ============ Download ============
async function downloadExcel() {
    if (!allVideos.length) { alert('暂无数据可下载'); return; }

    const btn = document.querySelector('.download-btn');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = '⏳ 生成 Excel...';

    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/download/excel', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({
                videos: allVideos,
                comments: commentAnalysisData || {},
                username: currentUsername,
                platform: currentPlatform,
            })
        });

        if (!resp.ok) {
            const err = await resp.json();
            alert('下载失败: ' + (err.error || resp.statusText));
            return;
        }

        const blob = await resp.blob();
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `达人分析_${currentUsername}_${currentPlatform}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.xlsx`;
        a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch(e) {
        alert('下载失败: ' + e.message);
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = '📥 下载完整分析数据 (Excel)';
    }
}

function escHtml(s) {
    if (s === null || s === undefined) return '';
    return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ============ Panel Navigation ============
function switchPanel(num) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.add('hidden'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('panel-' + num).classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('nav-' + num).classList.add('active');
    const subtitles = {
        1: '输入任意达人ID → 选题分布 + 双维度评论分析 → 定位细分赛道 + 评估购买意向',
        2: '输入任意 TikTok / Instagram 视频链接 → 口播全文 + HOOK/中段/CTA 拆解 + 全部评论',
        3: '上传包含视频链接的 Excel → 批量返回最新 View 数据'
    };
    document.getElementById('page-subtitle').textContent = subtitles[num];
}

// ============ Panel 2: Single Video Analysis ============
let videoAbortController = null;

async function analyzeSingleVideo() {
    const url = document.getElementById('video-url-input').value.trim();
    const platform = document.getElementById('video-platform-input').value;
    const language = document.getElementById('video-language-input').value;
    const status = document.getElementById('video-status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('video-analyze-btn');
    const resultsArea = document.getElementById('video-results-area');

    if (!url) { status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">请输入视频链接</span>'; return; }

    btn.disabled = true;
    resultsArea.classList.add('hidden');
    status.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 启动分析任务...';

    videoAbortController = new AbortController();

    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/video/analyze', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            signal: videoAbortController.signal,
            body: JSON.stringify({url, platform, language})
        });
        const initData = await resp.json();
        if (initData.error) { renderError('video-status', initData.error, 'ScrapeCreators'); btn.disabled = false; return; }

        const taskId = initData.task_id;
        let elapsed = 0;
        while (true) {
            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
            elapsed += 3;
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 分析中... (已等待 ${elapsed}s) <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="margin-left:8px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px" onclick="stopVideoAnalysis()">停止</button>`;

            const pollResp = await fetch('/api/video/status/' + taskId, {signal: videoAbortController.signal});
            if (videoAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
            const pollData = await pollResp.json();

            if (pollData.status === 'done') {
                status.innerHTML = '<span class="ok">✅ 视频分析完成</span>';
                renderSingleVideoResults(pollData.result);
                resultsArea.classList.remove('hidden');
                break;
            } else if (pollData.status === 'error') {
                renderError('video-status', pollData.error, 'ScrapeCreators');
                break;
            }
            if (elapsed > 180) {
                status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">分析超时（>180s），请重试</span>';
                break;
            }
        }
    } catch(e) {
        if (videoAbortController.signal.aborted) {
            status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">分析已停止</span>';
        } else {
            renderError('video-status', e.message || e, 'ScrapeCreators');
        }
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        videoAbortController = null;
    }
}

function stopVideoAnalysis() {
    if (videoAbortController) {
        videoAbortController.abort();
        videoAbortController = null;
    }
}

function renderSingleVideoResults(result) {
    const container = document.getElementById('video-results-area');
    const meta = result.metadata || {};
    const analysis = result.analysis || {};
    const comments = result.comments || [];
    const transcript = result.transcript || '（未获取到口播原文）';

    // Format numbers
    const fmt = n => n === null || n === undefined ? '-' : Number(n).toLocaleString();

    // Comments HTML
    let commentsHtml = '';
    if (comments.length) {
        commentsHtml = `<p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:8px">共 ${comments.length} 条有效评论</p>`;
        comments.forEach(c => {
            commentsHtml += `<div class="comment-item" style="padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed #e2e8f0">
                <span style="font-weight:600">@${escHtml(c.username || '匿名')}</span>
                <span style="color:#64748b;font-size:12px">👍 ${c.likes || 0}</span>
                <p style="margin-top:4px">${escHtml(c.text || '')}</p>
            </div>`;
        });
    } else {
        commentsHtml = '<p class="text-sm">未抓取到有效评论</p>';
    }

    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="section">
            <h2>📊 基础数据盘</h2>
            <div class="meta-grid">
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${fmt(meta.views)}</div><div class="label">播放量</div></div>
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${fmt(meta.likes)}</div><div class="label">点赞</div></div>
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${fmt(meta.comments)}</div><div class="label">评论</div></div>
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${fmt(meta.shares)}</div><div class="label">分享</div></div>
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${escHtml(meta.author || '-')}</div><div class="label">达人</div></div>
                <div class="meta-item"><div class="num">${meta.platform || '-'}</div><div class="label">平台</div></div>
            </div>
            <p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b"><strong>标题：</strong>${escHtml(meta.title || '-')}</p>
            <p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-top:4px"><strong>链接：</strong><a href="${escHtml(meta.url || '')}" class="video-link" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escHtml(meta.url || '')}</a></p>
        </div>

        <div class="section">
            <h2>📝 口播原文</h2>
            <div class="transcript-box">${escHtml(transcript)}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="section">
            <h2>🧠 内容拆解（DeepSeek）</h2>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>🎯 HOOK（前 3 秒）</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.hook || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>📝 中段内容</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.middle || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>📢 CTA / 结尾引导</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.cta || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>💡 一句话总结</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.content_summary || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>👥 观众洞察</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.audience_insight || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="analysis-card">
                <h4>🔧 优化建议</h4>
                <p>${escHtml(analysis.improvement_suggestions || '暂无分析')}</p>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="section">
            <h2>💬 全部评论（最多 50 条）</h2>
            <div>${commentsHtml}</div>
        </div>
    `;
}

// ============ Panel 3: Bulk Views Update (Paste Mode) ============
let viewsAbortController = null;

async function fetchPasteViews() {
    const status = document.getElementById('views-status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('views-paste-btn');
    const resultsArea = document.getElementById('views-results-area');

    const tkRaw = document.getElementById('paste-tk-urls').value.trim();
    const igRaw = document.getElementById('paste-ig-urls').value.trim();

    if (!tkRaw && !igRaw) {
        status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">请至少在一个框中粘贴链接</span>';
        return;
    }

    btn.disabled = true;
    resultsArea.classList.add('hidden');
    status.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在提交...';

    viewsAbortController = new AbortController();

    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/videos/views/paste', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ tk_urls: tkRaw, ig_urls: igRaw }),
            signal: viewsAbortController.signal
        });
        const initData = await resp.json();
        if (initData.error) { renderError('views-status', initData.error, 'ScrapeCreators'); btn.disabled = false; return; }

        const taskId = initData.task_id;
        const total = initData.total || 0;
        let elapsed = 0;
        while (true) {
            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
            elapsed += 3;
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></span> 正在抓取 ${total} 行 View... (已等待 ${elapsed}s)`;

            const pollResp = await fetch('/api/videos/views/status/' + taskId, { signal: viewsAbortController.signal });
            if (viewsAbortController.signal.aborted) return;
            const pollData = await pollResp.json();

            if (pollData.status === 'done') {
                status.innerHTML = '<span class="ok">✅ View 抓取完成</span>';
                renderPasteViewsResults(pollData.result);
                resultsArea.classList.remove('hidden');
                break;
            } else if (pollData.status === 'error') {
                renderError('views-status', pollData.error, 'ScrapeCreators');
                break;
            }
            if (elapsed > 300) {
                status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">抓取超时（>300s），请重试</span>';
                break;
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        if (viewsAbortController.signal.aborted) {
            status.innerHTML = '<span class="error">已停止</span>';
        } else {
            status.innerHTML = `<span class="error">请求失败: ${e.message}</span>`;
        }
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        viewsAbortController = null;
    }
}

function renderPasteViewsResults(result) {
    const container = document.getElementById('views-results-area');
    const rows = result.rows || [];
    if (!rows.length) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="section"><p class="text-sm">无结果</p></div>';
        return;
    }

    const fmt = n => n === null || n === undefined ? '-' : Number(n).toLocaleString();
    let tkCopyData = [];
    let igCopyData = [];

    let html = `<div class="section"><h2>📊 View 数据（按行对齐）</h2>
        <p style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:8px">共 ${rows.length} 行。点击下方按钮一键复制整列，粘贴回 Excel 的 C 列 / D 列。</p>
        <table class="video-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>TK View</th><th>IG View</th><th>状态</th></tr></thead><tbody>`;

    rows.forEach((row, i) => {
        const tkViews = row.tk_view;
        const igViews = row.ig_view;
        const tkErr = row.tk_error;
        const igErr = row.ig_error;

        let tkDisplay = '-', igDisplay = '-';
        if (tkErr) { tkDisplay = `<span style="color:#dc2626;font-size:12px">⚠</span>`; tkCopyData.push('ERROR'); }
        else if (tkViews !== null && tkViews !== undefined) { tkDisplay = fmt(tkViews); tkCopyData.push(String(tkViews)); }
        else { tkCopyData.push(''); }

        if (igErr) { igDisplay = `<span style="color:#dc2626;font-size:12px">⚠</span>`; igCopyData.push('ERROR'); }
        else if (igViews !== null && igViews !== undefined) { igDisplay = fmt(igViews); igCopyData.push(String(igViews)); }
        else { igCopyData.push(''); }

        let statusText = '';
        if (tkErr && igErr) statusText = '<span style="color:#dc2626">TK+IG 均失败</span>';
        else if (tkErr) statusText = '<span style="color:#dc2626">TK 失败</span>';
        else if (igErr) statusText = '<span style="color:#dc2626">IG 失败</span>';
        else statusText = '<span style="color:#059669">OK</span>';

        html += `<tr>
            <td>${i + 1}</td>
            <td>${tkDisplay}</td>
            <td>${igDisplay}</td>
            <td>${statusText}</td>
        </tr>`;
    });
    html += '</tbody></table>';

    // 一键复制按钮
    html += `<div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center">
        <button class="btn" onclick="copyColumn('tk')" id="btn-copy-tk">📋 一键复制 TK View 列</button>
        <button class="btn" onclick="copyColumn('ig')" id="btn-copy-ig">📋 一键复制 IG View 列</button>
        <span class="text-sm" id="copy-msg"></span>
    </div>`;
    html += '</div>';
    container.innerHTML = html;

    // 保存复制数据
    window._tkCopyData = tkCopyData;
    window._igCopyData = igCopyData;
}

async function copyColumn(type) {
    const data = type === 'tk' ? window._tkCopyData : window._igCopyData;
    if (!data || !data.length) return;

    const text = data.join('\n');
    try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
        const msg = document.getElementById('copy-msg');
        const label = type === 'tk' ? 'TK View' : 'IG View';
        msg.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ ${label} 已复制（${data.length} 行），去 Excel 粘贴吧</span>`;
        setTimeout(() => { msg.innerHTML = ''; }, 3000);
    } catch (e) {
        // 回退方案：选中 textarea 手动复制
        const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
        ta.value = text;
        ta.style.position = 'fixed';
        ta.style.left = '-9999px';
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
        const msg = document.getElementById('copy-msg');
        msg.innerHTML = `<span class="ok">✅ 已复制，去 Excel 粘贴</span>`;
        setTimeout(() => { msg.innerHTML = ''; }, 3000);
    }
}

// ============ API Key Management ============
let apiKeyStatus = {};

async function loadApiKeyStatus() {
    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/config/keys');
        apiKeyStatus = await resp.json();
        updateSettingsUI();
    } catch(e) { console.error('load keys failed', e); }
}

async function loadCredits() {
    try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/config/credits');
        const credits = await resp.json();
        const sc = credits.scrapecreators;
        const ap = credits.apify;
        if (sc && sc.keys) {
            const parts = sc.keys.map(k => 
                `${k.masked}: ${k.status === 'active' ? '✅' : k.status === 'exhausted' ? '💰用完' : '❓'}`
            );
            document.getElementById('sc-credits').textContent = parts.join(' | ');
        } else {
            document.getElementById('sc-credits').textContent = '';
        }
        if (ap && !ap.error) {
            document.getElementById('apify-credits').textContent = 
                `剩余 $${ap.remaining?.toFixed(2) || '?'}`;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('apify-credits').textContent = '';
        }
        return {ok: true};
    } catch(e) { 
        console.error('load credits failed', e); 
        return {ok: false, error: e.message};
    }
}

async function refreshCredits() {
    const btn = document.querySelector('button[onclick="refreshCredits()"]');
    const originalText = btn.textContent;
    btn.textContent = '🔄 刷新中...';
    btn.disabled = true;
    
    const result = await loadCredits();
    
    btn.textContent = originalText;
    btn.disabled = false;
    
    const msg = document.getElementById('refresh-msg');
    if (!msg) {
        // 动态创建提示元素
        const hint = document.querySelector('.modal-actions');
        const span = document.createElement('span');
        span.id = 'refresh-msg';
        span.style.marginRight = '12px';
        span.style.fontSize = '13px';
        hint.insertBefore(span, hint.firstChild);
    }
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('refresh-msg');
    if (result.ok) {
        msgEl.innerHTML = '<span class="ok">✅ 刷新成功</span>';
    } else {
        msgEl.innerHTML = `<span class="error">❌ 刷新失败: ${escHtml(result.error)}</span>`;
    }
    setTimeout(() => { msgEl.textContent = ''; }, 3000);
}

function updateSettingsUI() {
    const setStatus = (id, info, extra) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!el || !info) return;
        let text = info.set ? `已设置 (${info.masked})` : '未设置';
        if (extra) text += ' ' + extra;
        el.textContent = text;
        el.className = 'key-status ' + (info.set ? 'ok' : 'missing');
    };
    const sc = apiKeyStatus.scrapecreators;
    const scExtra = (sc && sc.count > 1) ? `[${sc.count} 个 Key]` : '';
    setStatus('sc-status', sc, scExtra);
    setStatus('apify-status', apiKeyStatus.apify);
    setStatus('deepseek-status', apiKeyStatus.deepseek);
}

function openSettingsModal() {
    loadApiKeyStatus();
    loadCredits();
    document.getElementById('settings-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
}

function closeSettingsModal() {
    document.getElementById('settings-modal').classList.add('hidden');
}

async function saveApiKeys() {
    const scKey = document.getElementById('sc-key-input').value.trim();
    const apifyKey = document.getElementById('apify-key-input').value.trim();
    const deepseekKey = document.getElementById('deepseek-key-input').value.trim();

    const saveOne = async (endpoint, key) => {
        if (!key) return null;
        const resp = await fetch(endpoint, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({key})
        });
        return resp.json();
    };

    try {
        await Promise.all([
            scKey ? saveOne('/api/config/scrapecreators-key', scKey) : null,
            apifyKey ? saveOne('/api/config/apify-key', apifyKey) : null,
            deepseekKey ? saveOne('/api/config/deepseek-key', deepseekKey) : null,
        ]);
        await loadApiKeyStatus();
        alert('API Key 已更新');
        document.getElementById('sc-key-input').value = '';
        document.getElementById('apify-key-input').value = '';
        document.getElementById('deepseek-key-input').value = '';
        closeSettingsModal();
    } catch(e) {
        alert('保存失败: ' + e.message);
    }
}

// 检测错误类型
function classifyError(errText) {
    if (!errText) return {type: 'unknown'};
    const t = (typeof errText === 'string' ? errText : JSON.stringify(errText)).toLowerCase();
    if (t.includes('credit_exhausted') || t.includes('out of credits') || t.includes('not-enough-usage') || t.includes('额度已用完')) {
        return {type: 'credit_exhausted', label: '额度耗尽'};
    }
    if (t.includes('scrapecreators_404') || (t.includes('404') && (t.includes('无法访问') || t.includes('私密') || t.includes('删除')))) {
        return {type: 'video_404', label: '视频无法访问'};
    }
    if (t.includes('rate_limited') || t.includes('过于频繁')) {
        return {type: 'rate_limited', label: '请求过频'};
    }
    if (t.includes('auth_failed') || t.includes('401') || t.includes('key 无效') || t.includes('key 无效')) {
        return {type: 'auth_failed', label: 'Key 无效'};
    }
    if (t.includes('402')) return {type: 'credit_exhausted', label: '额度耗尽'};
    return {type: 'unknown', label: '未知错误'};
}

function isCreditError(errText) {
    return classifyError(errText).type === 'credit_exhausted';
}

// 统一显示错误，自动识别错误类型并给出对应操作入口
function renderError(containerId, message, serviceName) {
    const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if (!container) return;
    const msgStr = typeof message === 'string' ? message : JSON.stringify(message);
    const errType = classifyError(msgStr);

    switch (errType.type) {
        case 'credit_exhausted':
            container.innerHTML = `<span class="error">💰 ${serviceName || 'API'} 额度已用完</span> 
                <button class="btn" style="margin-left:8px;padding:4px 12px;font-size:12px" onclick="openSettingsModal()">⚙️ 输入新 Key</button>
                <div class="text-sm" style="margin-top:4px">${escHtml(msgStr.substring(0, 200))}</div>`;
            break;
        case 'video_404':
            container.innerHTML = `<span class="error">🔒 视频无法访问</span>
                <div style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-top:4px">该视频可能为私密账号、已删除或存在地区限制。请确认链接在浏览器中可正常打开后重试。</div>
                <div class="text-sm" style="margin-top:4px">${escHtml(msgStr.substring(0, 200))}</div>`;
            break;
        case 'rate_limited':
            container.innerHTML = `<span class="error">⏳ 请求过于频繁</span>
                <div style="font-size:13px;color:#64748b;margin-top:4px">请稍等 10-30 秒后重试。</div>`;
            break;
        case 'auth_failed':
            container.innerHTML = `<span class="error">🔑 ${serviceName || 'API'} Key 无效</span>
                <button class="btn" style="margin-left:8px;padding:4px 12px;font-size:12px" onclick="openSettingsModal()">⚙️ 检查 Key</button>`;
            break;
        default:
            container.innerHTML = `<span class="error">${escHtml(msgStr.substring(0, 300))}</span>`;
    }
}

// ============ Init ============
// Page loads with empty state — user must input influencer
document.getElementById('results-area').classList.add('hidden');
switchPanel(1);
loadApiKeyStatus();
</script>
</body>
</html>

```

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>

<title>达人分析系统 — API 工具评估报告 v2（大白话版）</title>
<style>
*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
body{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI","PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif;line-height:1.9;color:#1a1a2e;background:#f0f2f5;padding:24px}
.container{max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto}

h1{font-size:28px;margin-bottom:4px;color:#0f172a}
h2{font-size:22px;color:#1e293b;margin:36px 0 16px;padding-bottom:8px;border-bottom:3px solid #4472C4}

.subtitle{color:#64748b;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:32px}

.card{background:#fff;border-radius:14px;padding:28px;margin-bottom:24px;box-shadow:0 2px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)}

table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:14px}
th{background:#1e293b;color:#fff;padding:12px 14px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;font-size:13px}

.big-num{font-size:32px;font-weight:800;color:#4472C4;line-height:1.2}

.conclusion-box{background:#f0fdf4;border:2px solid #22c55e;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;margin:20px 0}

.step{display:flex;gap:16px;margin:16px 0;align-items:flex-start}
.step-num{background:#4472C4;color:#fff;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:800;font-size:16px;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px}
.step-body{flex:1}

.qa{margin:16px 0}

.highlight{background:#fffbeb;border-left:4px solid #f59e0b;padding:16px 20px;border-radius:4px;margin:16px 0;font-size:14px}

</style>
</head>
<body>

<h1>📊 API 工具评估报告 v2</h1>

<!-- ============ 一张表说清楚 ============ -->
<div class="card">

<table>

<td><strong>IG 抓评论成功率</strong></td>
<td>~90%（10次失败1次）</td>
<td>高</td>
<td>高</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>最低月费</strong></td>
<td>$10 ≈ ¥72</td>
<td>£9 ≈ ¥80</td>
<td>$49 ≈ ¥350</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>适合谁</strong></td>
<td><span class="tag tag-win">所有人</span></td>
<td>不在乎钱的人</td>
<td>只抓 IG 且要原生排序</td>
</tr>
</table>
</div>

<!-- ============ 大白话场景对比 ============ -->
<div class="card">
<h2>🎬 大白话场景：同一条视频，花一样的钱</h2>

<h3>场景：我要分析一个达人的一条 IG 视频评论</h3>

<div class="compare-box">
<div class="compare-card winner">
<h4>🏆 ScrapeCreators</h4>
<div class="num" style="color:#059669">75-150 条</div>
<div class="desc">花 5 credits<br>调 1 次 + 翻 4 页</div>
<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:#64748b">量大管饱，本地排个序<br>高赞低赞全都有</div>
</div>
<div class="compare-card loser">
<h4>SocialCrawl</h4>
<div class="num" style="color:#dc2626">10-20 条</div>

<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:#64748b">条条高赞，但就这点<br>长尾评论完全看不到</div>
</div>
<div class="compare-card loser">
<h4>Apify（免费）</h4>
<div class="num" style="color:#dc2626">10 条</div>
<div class="desc">花 $0.005<br>随机 10 条</div>
<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:#64748b">免费的只有 10 条<br>还不能挑，随机的</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="conclusion-box">
<h3>💡 大白话翻译</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>SocialCrawl</strong>：去餐厅点菜，只能点一道，好吃但吃不饱</li>
<li><strong>Apify 免费</strong>：免费试吃，就给一小口，还随机上菜</li>
<li><strong>ScrapeCreators</strong>：自助餐，花一样的钱随便拿，吃完了还能再拿</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>

<!-- ============ 实际业务能解决什么问题 ============ -->
<div class="card">
<h2>💼 实际业务：分别能解决什么问题</h2>

<h3>你的核心需求回顾</h3>
<ul style="margin-left:20px;font-size:14px">
<li>每达人分析 6 条视频（TOP-3 热门 + TOP-3 赞助）</li>
<li>每条视频至少 20 条评论</li>
<li>需要看到评论分类的完整分布（不只是高赞）</li>
<li>校准分类准确率到 90%+</li>
</ul>

<hr>

<h3>用 ScrapeCreators 免费版</h3>
<table>
<tr><th>能做什么</th><th>不能做什么</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>✅ 测 80-100 个视频评论<br>✅ 每视频 15-30 条，不够就翻页<br>✅ 校准 10-15 个达人够用<br>✅ 长尾评论也抓得到，分类校准更准</td>
<td>⚠️ 100 credits 用完就没了<br>⚠️ IG 有 ~10% 概率失败，重试即可</td>
</tr>
</table>

<h3>用 ScrapeCreators Solo Dev（$10/月）</h3>
<table>
<tr><th>能做什么</th><th>不能做什么</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>✅ 每月分析 150+ 达人<br>✅ 每条视频翻 2 页拿 30-60 条评论<br>✅ 评论量大，校准样本足<br>✅ 本地排序，高赞照样筛得出</td>
<td>⚠️ 还是没有原生 sort=top<br>（但大样本 + 本地排 = 效果一样）</td>
</tr>
</table>

<h3>用 SocialCrawl（同样花 ~$10）</h3>
<table>
<tr><th>能做什么</th><th>不能做什么</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>✅ 原生高赞排序<br>✅ IG 成功率稳定</td>
<td>❌ 只能测 20 个视频<br>❌ 不够校准用<br>❌ 看不到长尾评论<br>❌ 花同样的钱，量少 7 倍</td>
</tr>
</table>
</div>

能测 80-100 个视频，校准 10-15 个达人完全够<br>
<strong>成本：¥0</strong></p>

每月分析 150+ 达人，单视频翻页拿更多评论<br>

每月分析 800+ 达人，评论量管够<br>

<div class="highlight" style="margin-top:20px">
<strong>⚠️ 什么时候才需要考虑换工具？</strong><br>
只有一种情况：你对 IG 评论的 90% 成功率无法容忍，且预算充裕。那时可以切 Apify Starter（$49/月 + 评论按量），用它的 sortOrder=popular。但绝大多数场景下不值得。
</div>

<!-- ============ QA ============ -->
<div class="card">
<h2>❓ 你可能还想问</h2>

<div class="qa">
<div class="q">Q: ScrapeCreators 没有 sort=top，怎么保证抓到高赞评论？</div>
<div class="a">多抓 + 本地排序。一条视频抓 50-100 条评论，按 likes 降序排，Top 20 的高赞程度和 SocialCrawl 原生返回的几乎一样。你样本大了，自然覆盖到高赞。SocialCrawl 只是帮你筛好了，但筛完只剩 15 条。</div>
</div>

<div class="qa">
<div class="q">Q: IG 评论 90% 成功率影响大吗？</div>
<div class="a">不大。失败就重试一次，多花 1 credit。100 次里失败 10 次，重试 10 次，多花 10 credits。和 SocialCrawl 每条多花 4 credits 相比，这点损耗不值一提。</div>
</div>

<div class="qa">
<div class="q">Q: Apify 真的完全不能用吗？</div>
<div class="a">不是不能用，是门槛太高。免费版限 10 条/次等于废了，付费要先交 $49 月费才解锁正常用量。同样 $49，ScrapeCreators Freelance 能抓 5000 个视频。除非你对原生排序有执念，否则没必要。</div>
</div>

<div class="qa">
<div class="q">Q: 标题抓取用谁？</div>
<div class="a">标题和评论分开。标题用 Apify 免费 $5/月（actor sones/instagram-posts-scraper-lowcost，$1.50/千条），评论用 ScrapeCreators。各取所长。</div>
</div>
</div>

<!DOCTYPE html>

    <title>TK & IG 达人筛选系统 - 方法论文档 v1.0</title>
    <style>
        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
        body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 1.8; color: #333; background: #f5f7fa; padding: 20px; }

        .subtitle { color: #666; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 32px; }
        h2 { color: #1a1a2e; font-size: 24px; margin-top: 40px; margin-bottom: 16px; padding-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #e8ecf1; }
        h3 { color: #2d3748; font-size: 18px; margin-top: 24px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
        .highlight { background: #fff3cd; border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b; padding: 16px 20px; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 6px; }
        .success { background: #d1fae5; border-left: 4px solid #10b981; padding: 16px 20px; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 6px; }
        .info { background: #dbeafe; border-left: 4px solid #3b82f6; padding: 16px 20px; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 6px; }
        table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 20px 0; font-size: 14px; }
        th { background: #1e293b; color: #fff; padding: 12px 14px; text-align: left; font-weight: 600; }
        td { padding: 12px 14px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; vertical-align: top; }
        tr:nth-child(even) { background: #f8fafc; }
        .badge { display: inline-block; padding: 3px 12px; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; margin-right: 4px; }
        .badge-green { background: #d1fae5; color: #065f46; }
        .badge-yellow { background: #fef3c7; color: #92400e; }
        .badge-red { background: #fee2e2; color: #991b1b; }
        .badge-blue { background: #dbeafe; color: #1e40af; }
        ul, ol { margin: 12px 0 12px 24px; }
        li { margin: 6px 0; }
        .grid-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 24px; margin: 20px 0; }
        .card { background: #f8fafc; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; }
        .card h4 { margin-bottom: 12px; color: #4472C4; }
        .stat-row { display: flex; gap: 20px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin: 20px 0; }
        .stat-item { background: #f0f4ff; border-radius: 8px; padding: 16px 20px; text-align: center; min-width: 140px; }
        .stat-item .num { font-size: 28px; font-weight: 700; color: #4472C4; }
        .stat-item .label { font-size: 13px; color: #64748b; margin-top: 4px; }
        .footer { margin-top: 40px; padding-top: 20px; border-top: 1px solid #e2e8f0; color: #64748b; font-size: 13px; }
